  var I = rgb(T.ink), A = rgb(T.accent), H = rgb(T.hot), K = rgb(T.cta);
  function lum(hex) {
    return rgb(hex).split(",").map(function (c) { c = c / 255; return c <= 0.03928 ? c / 12.92 : Math.pow((c + 0.055) / 1.055, 2.4); })
      .reduce(function (s, c, i) { return s + c * [0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722][i]; }, 0);
  }
  function dist(a, b) { var x = rgb(a).split(","), y = rgb(b).split(","); return Math.sqrt(x.reduce(function (s, v, i) { return s + Math.pow(v - y[i], 2); }, 0)); }
  var DARK = lum(T.ink) > lum(T.paper); /* dark template: light text on a dark ground */
  var DK = DARK ? T.paper : T.ink, LT = DARK ? T.ink : T.paper;
  var ALT = !!T.altFill || dist(T.cta, T.ink) < 60; /* ink-coloured primary → All-Access takes the accent */
  var NM = dist(T.mark || T.cta, T.accentText || T.accent) < 60 ? T.ink : (T.accentText || T.accent); /* the name sits on the mark band — never in the band's colour */

    "--acc-text:" + (T.accentText || T.accent) + ";--acc-ink:" + (T.accentInk || "#fff") + ";--mark:" + (T.mark || T.cta) + ";--mark-rgb:" + rgb(T.mark || T.cta) + ";--label:" + (T.label || T.body) + ";--num:" + (T.num || T.display) + ";--nw:" + (T.numWeight || T.displayWeight || 650) + ";",

    "@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*,*::before,*::after{animation:none!important;transition-duration:.01ms!important}}"
  ].join("");

  function roll(label, short) {
    return '<span class="rl' + (short ? " lg" : "") + '"><span>' + esc(label) + '</span><span aria-hidden="true">' + esc(label) + "</span></span>" +
      (short ? '<span class="sm">' + esc(short) + "</span>" : "");
  }

  function markup() {
    var t = C.tiers;
    var opts = ORDER.map(function (k, i) {
      var p = PLAN[k];
      return '<button class="op" type="button" role="radio" aria-checked="false" data-k="' + k + '" style="--i:' + i + '">' +
        '<span class="rd" aria-hidden="true"></span>' +
        '<span class="ot"><b>' + esc(p.name) + (p.flag ? '<em class="stk">' + esc(p.flag) + "</em>" : "") + "</b><small>" + esc(p.sub) + "</small></span>" +
        '<span class="pr">' + (k === "all" ? '<s class="aa-was">' + money(AA.value) + "</s>" : "") + (k === "cust" ? '<b data-kup="1">from ' + money(CUST_FROM) + "</b>" : "<b>" + (k === "free" ? "Free" : money(t[k].price)) + "</b>") + "</span></button>";
    }).join("");
    var collage = (C.collage || []).slice(0, 4).map(function (u) { return '<img alt="" decoding="async" data-src="' + esc(u) + '">'; }).join("");
    var stats = (C.stats || []).map(function (s) { return '<div class="stat"><b data-to="' + esc(s[0]) + '">' + esc(s[0]) + "</b><span>" + esc(s[1]) + "</span></div>"; }).join("");
    var best = (C.bestFor || []).map(function (b) { return "<span>" + esc(b) + "</span>"; }).join("");
    var n = 0, featCount = 0;
    (C.features || []).forEach(function (g) { featCount += g.items.length; });
    var feats = (C.features || []).map(function (g) {
      return '<div class="fg2 rv"><h4>' + esc(STAR) + " " + esc(g.g) + '</h4><ul class="fl3">' + g.items.map(function (f) {
        n += 1;
        return '<li><span class="ib">' + svg(f[0]) + "</span><div><b><i>" + pad(n) + "</i>" + esc(f[1]) + "</b><p>" + esc(f[2]) + "</p></div></li>";
      }).join("") + "</ul></div>";
    }).join("");
    var bonus = BONUS.map(function (b) {
      return '<li><span class="ib">' + svg(b[0]) + "</span><span><b>" + esc(b[1]) + "</b><small>" + esc(b[2]) + '</small></span><em class="val">$' + b[3] + " value</em></li>";
    }).join("");
    var steps = STEPS.map(function (s, i) { return '<li><span class="no">' + (i + 1) + "</span><div><b>" + fill(s[0]) + "</b><p>" + fill(s[1]) + "</p></div></li>"; }).join("");
    var every = EVERY.map(function (e) { return '<li><span class="ck">' + svg("check") + "</span><span>" + esc(e) + "</span></li>"; }).join("");
    var faq = FAQ.map(function (q, i) {
      return '<div class="qa"><button type="button" data-q="' + i + '" aria-expanded="false">' + esc(q[0]) + '<i aria-hidden="true">+</i></button><div class="an"><div><p>' + esc(q[1]) + "</p></div></div></div>";
    }).join("");
    var pages = (C.pages || []).map(function (p) { return "<span>" + esc(p) + "</span>"; }).join("");
    var ask = "mailto:" + EMAIL + "?subject=" + encodeURIComponent("Question about " + N) +
      "&body=" + encodeURIComponent("Hi, I'm looking at " + N + " and have a question:\n\n");

    return '<div class="st' + (ALT ? " alt" : "") + '" role="region" aria-label="Get ' + esc(N) + '">' +
        '<button class="sb sb-get" type="button" data-open="get" aria-haspopup="dialog"><i class="fx" aria-hidden="true"></i>' +
          '<span class="lb">' + roll("Get template") + '</span><span class="pc">' + (FREE ? "Free" : "from " + money(t.standard.price)) + "</span></button>" +
        '<div class="sr">' +
          '<button class="sb sb-all" type="button" data-open="all" aria-haspopup="dialog"><i class="fx" aria-hidden="true"></i>' + roll("Get All-Access") + '<span class="pc">' + money(t.all.price) + "</span></button>" +
          '<a class="sb sb-hire" href="' + esc(track((C.ku && C.ku.page) || C.hireUrl, "cust")) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><i class="fx" aria-hidden="true"></i>' +
            '<span class="lb"><i class="pd" aria-hidden="true"></i>' + roll("Customization") + '</span><span class="pc" data-kup="1">from ' + money(CUST_FROM) + "</span></a>" +
        "</div>" +
        '<button class="sx" type="button" aria-label="Hide these buttons">×</button>' +
      "</div>" +
      '<button class="mini" type="button" hidden>' + esc(STAR) + " Get <b>" + esc(N) + "</b></button>" +
      '<div class="ov" hidden>' +
        '<div class="bd" data-x></div>' +
        '<div class="cd" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="sft">' +
          '<div class="hd"><div><p class="ey">SoloFoundry ' + esc(STAR) + ' Framer template</p><h2 id="sft">Get <span class="nmx">' + esc(N) + "</span></h2>" +
            '<p class="su">Pick how you’ll use it — everything else is included.</p></div>' +
            '<button class="xx" type="button" data-x aria-label="Close">×</button><span class="prog" aria-hidden="true"><i></i></span></div>' +
          '<div class="sc">' +
            '<div class="opts" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Licence">' + opts + "</div>" +
            (FREE ? '<div class="nudge" hidden><span>Want the premium ones too? <b>All-Access unlocks every template</b> — ' + money(t.all.price) + ' once.</span><button type="button" data-pick="all">See All-Access →</button></div>' : '<div class="nudge" hidden><span>Building for clients? <b>Pro is only ' + money(t.pro.price - t.standard.price) + ' more</b> — unlimited websites' + (KIT_ALL ? "" : " + the bonus kit") + '.</span><button type="button" data-pick="pro">Switch to Pro →</button></div>') +
            '<div class="pv rv"><img class="pv-one" alt="' + esc(N) + ' preview" decoding="async" data-src="' + esc(C.image) + '"><div class="pv-all">' + collage + '</div><span class="pvt"></span></div>' +
            '<ul class="rows"></ul>' +
            '<div class="blk2 aal" hidden><div class="sec-h"><h3>Everything in All-Access</h3><span>50+ templates · paid + free</span></div><div class="aag"></div><a class="more" href="' + esc(track(C.browseUrl, "browse_all")) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Browse all templates <i aria-hidden="true">→</i></a></div>' +
            (stats ? '<div class="blk2 rv stats-w"><div class="stats">' + stats + "</div></div>" : "") +
            '<div class="blk2 rv about"><div class="sec-h"><h3>About ' + esc(N) + "</h3><span>" + esc(C.trade || "") + "</span></div><p>" + esc(C.about || "") + "</p>" +
              (best ? '<div class="bf"><small>Built for</small><div class="chips">' + best + "</div></div>" : "") + "</div>" +
            (feats ? '<div class="blk2 feats"><div class="sec-h rv"><h3>What’s inside ' + esc(N) + "</h3><span>" + featCount + " features</span></div>" + feats + "</div>" : "") +
            (pages ? '<div class="blk2 pg rv"><div class="sec-h"><h3>Every page, ready to go</h3><span>' + (C.pages || []).length + " pages</span></div><div class=\"chips\">" + pages + "</div></div>" : "") +
            '<div class="blk2 rv"><div class="bonus"><p class="bh">' + (FREE ? "Bonus kit with All-Access" : KIT_ALL ? "Bonus kit" : "Bonus kit with Pro and All-Access") + ' <em class="stk">' + money(BONUS_TOTAL) + (KIT_ALL && !FREE ? " value · free" : " value") + "</em></p><ul>" + bonus + "</ul></div></div>" +
            '<div class="blk2 rv steps-w"><div class="sec-h"><h3>' + (FREE ? "From demo to live" : "From checkout to live") + '</h3><span>3 steps</span></div><ol class="steps">' + steps + "</ol></div>" +
            '<div class="blk2 rv"><div class="sec-h"><h3>Always included</h3></div><ul class="ev">' + every + "</ul></div>" +
            '<div class="blk2 rv faq-w"><div class="sec-h"><h3>Questions, answered</h3></div><div class="faq">' + faq + "</div></div>" +
            '<p class="fine rv">' + (FREE ? "The free remix asks for no email. All-Access sales are final — every project is delivered the moment you pay, so take the demos for a spin first." : "All sales are final — you get the complete Framer project the moment you pay, so take the demo for a spin first.") + ' <a href="' + esc(ask) + '">' + (FREE ? "Ask a question" : "Ask a question before you buy") + '</a></p>' +
          "</div>" +
          '<div class="ft"><p class="bs">' + esc(STAR) + " Bonus kit included <b>" + money(BONUS_TOTAL) + " value — free</b></p>" +
            '<a class="cta" target="_blank" rel="noopener" href="#"><i class="fx" aria-hidden="true"></i><span class="ct"></span>' + svg("arrow", "ia") + "</a>" +
            '<p class="sec">' + svg("lock", "il") + '<span class="sct">Secure checkout by Lemon Squeezy · opens in a new tab</span></p></div>' +
        "</div>" +
      "</div>";
  }

  /* ── Customization card ─────────────────────────────────────
     A second dialog, entirely its own: nothing here shares a name
     with the licence card's open/close/render/pick. */
  function loadFonts() {
    /* the template already loads its own fonts; optional extra stylesheet per theme */
    if (!T.fontCss || document.querySelector('link[data-sf-fonts]')) return;
    var l = document.createElement("link");
    l.rel = "stylesheet"; l.href = T.fontCss; l.setAttribute("data-sf-fonts", "");
    document.head.appendChild(l);
  }

  function magnet(el, strength) {
    if (!finePointer || reduce) return;
    function at(e) {
      var r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      el.style.setProperty("--fx", (e.clientX - r.left) + "px");
      el.style.setProperty("--fy", (e.clientY - r.top) + "px");
      return r;
    }
    el.addEventListener("pointerenter", at);
    el.addEventListener("pointermove", function (e) {
      var r = at(e);
      el.style.setProperty("--mx", ((e.clientX - r.left - r.width / 2) / r.width * strength).toFixed(2) + "px");
      el.style.setProperty("--my", ((e.clientY - r.top - r.height / 2) / r.height * strength).toFixed(2) + "px");
    });
    el.addEventListener("pointerleave", function (e) {
      at(e);
      el.style.setProperty("--mx", "0px");
      el.style.setProperty("--my", "0px");
    });
  }

  function countUp(el) {
    var to = parseInt(el.getAttribute("data-to"), 10);
    if (!to || reduce) return;
    var t0 = null, dur = 1100;
    function step(ts) {
      if (t0 === null) t0 = ts;
      var k = Math.min(1, (ts - t0) / dur), e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3);
      el.textContent = String(Math.round(to * e));
      if (k < 1) requestAnimationFrame(step);
    }
    el.textContent = "0";
    requestAnimationFrame(step);
  }

  function build() {
    loadFonts();
    var host = document.createElement("div");
    host.id = "sf-demo-bar";
    host.setAttribute("data-lenis-prevent", "");
    host.style.cssText = "position:fixed!important;left:0!important;top:0!important;width:0!important;height:0!important;z-index:2147483647!important;display:block!important;";
    var root = host.attachShadow({ mode: "open" });
    if (CUST && C.ku.priceUrl && window.fetch) { try { fetch(C.ku.priceUrl, { mode: "cors", cache: "no-cache" }).then(function (r) { return r.ok ? r.json() : null }).then(function (j) { var v = j && +j.customizationFrom; if (v > 0 && v !== CUST_FROM) { CUST_FROM = v; [].forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll("[data-kup]"), function (e) { e.textContent = "from " + money(v) }) } }).catch(function () {}) } catch (e) {} }
    root.innerHTML = "<style>" + CSS + "</style>" + markup();
    document.body.appendChild(host);

    /* keep page smooth-scroll libraries from hijacking the card's own scroll */
    ["wheel", "touchmove"].forEach(function (ev) {
      host.addEventListener(ev, function (e) { e.stopPropagation(); }, { passive: true });
    });

    function $(s) { return root.querySelector(s); }
    var st = $(".st"), mini = $(".mini"), ov = $(".ov"), cd = $(".cd"), sc = $(".sc"), prog = $(".prog i");
    var nudge = $(".nudge"), pv = $(".pv"), pvt = $(".pvt"), rows = $(".rows"), cta = $(".cta"), ct = $(".ct");
    var aal = $(".aal"), aag = $(".aag");
    var sel = "all", lastFocus = null, locked = null, closing = 0, imgs = false, ticking = false, showAll = false;

    [].forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll(".sb"), function (b) { magnet(b, 7); });
    magnet(cta, 5);

    function setCta(label) {
      cta.setAttribute("aria-label", label);
      var cur = ct.querySelector(".cur");
      if (cur && cur.textContent === label) return;
      var nu = document.createElement("span");
      nu.className = "cur" + (cur && !reduce ? " in" : "");
      nu.textContent = label;
      if (cur) {
        cur.className = "out";
        setTimeout(function () { if (cur.parentNode) cur.parentNode.removeChild(cur); }, reduce ? 0 : 330);
      }
      ct.appendChild(nu);
    }
    function renderAA() {
      /* a few names, never a count — the shelf grows every month */
      var list = AA.list.length ? AA.list : (C.aaNames || []);
      aal.hidden = !(sel === "all" && list.length);
      if (aal.hidden) return;
      var shown = list.filter(function (t) { return t.name !== N; }).slice(0, 7);
      shown.unshift({ name: N, category: "You're looking at it" });
      aag.innerHTML = shown.map(function (t, i) {
        return '<div class="' + (t.name === N ? "me" : "") + '" style="--i:' + i + '"><b>' + esc(t.name) + "</b><small>" + esc(t.category || "") + "</small></div>";
      }).join("");
    }
    function render() {
      [].forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll(".op"), function (el) {
        var on = el.getAttribute("data-k") === sel;
        el.classList.toggle("on", on);
        el.setAttribute("aria-checked", on ? "true" : "false");
        el.tabIndex = on ? 0 : -1;
      });
      nudge.hidden = sel !== (FREE ? "free" : "standard");
      pv.classList.toggle("all", sel === "all");
      pvt.textContent = PLAN[sel].tag;
      rows.innerHTML = rowsFor(sel).map(function (r, i) {
        return '<li style="--i:' + i + '"><span class="ib">' + svg(r[0]) + "</span><span>" + esc(r[1]) + "</span></li>";
      }).join("");
      renderAA();
      setCta(sel === "free" ? "Get " + N + " — Free" : sel === "cust" ? "See Customization — from " + money(CUST_FROM) : "Get " + PLAN[sel].name + " — " + money(C.tiers[sel].price));
      cta.href = sel === "cust" ? track(C.ku.page, "cust_card") : track(C.tiers[sel].url, sel);
      root.querySelector(".bs").hidden = !hasKit(sel); /* the kit line follows the picked plan */
      /* the bonus kit is delivered by the paid checkout only — never promise it next to the free remix */
      root.querySelector(".sct").textContent = sel === "cust" ? "Opens the three setup packages · new tab" : sel === "free" ? "Opens the remix in Framer · new tab · no email" : "Secure checkout by Lemon Squeezy · opens in a new tab";
    }
    function pick(k, focus) {
      sel = k;
      render();
      if (focus) { var el = $('.op[data-k="' + k + '"]'); if (el) el.focus(); }
    }

    var io = ("IntersectionObserver" in window) ? new IntersectionObserver(function (es) {
      es.forEach(function (en) {
        if (!en.isIntersecting) return;
        en.target.classList.add("in");
        io.unobserve(en.target);
        if (en.target.classList.contains("stats-w")) [].forEach.call(en.target.querySelectorAll("b[data-to]"), countUp);
      });
    }, { root: sc, threshold: 0.1 }) : null;
    function reveal() {
      [].forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll(".rv"), function (el) {
        if (!io || reduce) { el.classList.add("in"); return; }
        el.classList.remove("in");
        io.unobserve(el);
        io.observe(el);
      });
    }
    function progress() {
      ticking = false;
      var max = sc.scrollHeight - sc.clientHeight;
      prog.style.setProperty("--p", max > 0 ? (sc.scrollTop / max).toFixed(3) : "0");
    }
    sc.addEventListener("scroll", function () { if (!ticking) { ticking = true; requestAnimationFrame(progress); } }, { passive: true });

    function lock(on) {
      var de = document.documentElement, b = document.body;
      if (on && !locked) {
        locked = [de.style.overflow, b.style.overflow];
        de.style.overflow = "hidden"; b.style.overflow = "hidden";
        try { if (window.lenis && window.lenis.stop) window.lenis.stop(); } catch (e) {}
      } else if (!on && locked) {
        de.style.overflow = locked[0]; b.style.overflow = locked[1]; locked = null;
        try { if (window.lenis && window.lenis.start) window.lenis.start(); } catch (e) {}
      }
    }
    function open(from) {
      clearTimeout(closing);
      lastFocus = from || null;
      if (host.nextElementSibling) document.body.appendChild(host); /* stay above anything added later */
      if (!imgs) {
        imgs = true;
        [].forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll("img[data-src]"), function (im) { im.src = im.getAttribute("data-src"); });
      }
      sel = (CUST && from && from.getAttribute && from.getAttribute("data-open") === "get") ? "cust" : "all"; /* Get template → Customization; Get All-Access → All-Access */
      showAll = false;
      [].forEach.call(root.querySelectorAll(".qa.open"), function (q) { q.classList.remove("open"); q.firstChild.setAttribute("aria-expanded", "false"); });
      render();
      ov.hidden = false;
      sc.scrollTop = 0;
      /* on a short phone the 4th option sits below the fold: bring the preselected one into view */
      var onEl = $(".op.on");
      if (onEl && onEl.offsetTop + onEl.offsetHeight > sc.clientHeight - 18) sc.scrollTop = onEl.offsetTop + onEl.offsetHeight - sc.clientHeight + 30;
      progress();
      lock(true);
      requestAnimationFrame(function () {
        requestAnimationFrame(function () {
          ov.classList.add("open");
          reveal();
          try { $(".op.on").focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) {}
        });
      });
    }
    function close() {
      ov.classList.remove("open");
      closing = setTimeout(function () {
        ov.hidden = true;
        lock(false);
        if (lastFocus) { try { lastFocus.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) {} }
      }, reduce ? 0 : 420);
    }
    function collapse(on, keep) {
      st.hidden = on;
      mini.hidden = !on;
      if (!on) st.classList.add("in");
      if (keep !== false) ss("sfbar4-min", on ? "1" : "0");
    }
    function shelf() {
      function apply(S) {
        if (!S || !S.ALLACCESS || !S.ALLACCESS.value) return;
        AA.count = S.ALLACCESS.count || AA.count;
        AA.value = S.ALLACCESS.value;
        AA.list = (S.TEMPLATES || []).map(function (t) { return { name: t.name, category: t.category }; });
        var w = $(".aa-was");
        if (w) w.textContent = money(AA.value);
        if (!ov.hidden) render(); else renderAA();
      }
      if (window.SF && window.SF.ALLACCESS) { apply(window.SF); return; }
      var s = document.createElement("script");
      s.src = C.dataJs;
      s.async = true;
      s.onload = function () { try { apply(window.SF); } catch (e) {} };
      document.head.appendChild(s);
    }

    root.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      var el = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest("[data-open],[data-x],[data-pick],[data-k],[data-more],[data-q],.sx,.mini") : null;
      if (!el) return;
      if (el.hasAttribute("data-open")) open(el);
      else if (el.hasAttribute("data-x")) close();
      else if (el.hasAttribute("data-pick")) pick(el.getAttribute("data-pick"));
      else if (el.hasAttribute("data-k")) pick(el.getAttribute("data-k"));
      else if (el.hasAttribute("data-more")) { showAll = true; renderAA(); }
      else if (el.hasAttribute("data-q")) {
        var q = el.parentNode, isOpen = q.classList.toggle("open");
        el.setAttribute("aria-expanded", isOpen ? "true" : "false");
      }
      else if (el.classList.contains("sx")) collapse(true);
      else if (el.classList.contains("mini")) collapse(false);
    });
    $(".opts").addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
      var i = ORDER.indexOf(sel), n = null;
      if (e.key === "ArrowDown" || e.key === "ArrowRight") n = ORDER[(i + 1) % ORDER.length];
      if (e.key === "ArrowUp" || e.key === "ArrowLeft") n = ORDER[(i + ORDER.length - 1) % ORDER.length];
      if (n) { e.preventDefault(); pick(n, true); }
    });
    root.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
      if (ov.hidden || e.key !== "Tab") return;
      var f = [].slice.call(cd.querySelectorAll("button,a[href]")).filter(function (x) { return x.offsetParent !== null && x.tabIndex !== -1; });
      if (!f.length) return;
      var first = f[0], last = f[f.length - 1], act = root.activeElement;
      if (e.shiftKey && act === first) { e.preventDefault(); last.focus(); }
      else if (!e.shiftKey && act === last) { e.preventDefault(); first.focus(); }
    });
    document.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && !ov.hidden) { e.preventDefault(); close(); }
    });

    render();
    var wait = Math.max(900, 3000 - (window.performance && performance.now ? performance.now() : 0));
    /* C.hold = a full-screen intro (CAIRN's first-visit door): the stack waits until it has gone */
    function holding() {
      var h = C.hold && document.querySelector(C.hold);
      if (!h) return false;
      var r = h.getBoundingClientRect(), s = getComputedStyle(h);
      return r.width > 0 && r.height > 0 && s.display !== "none" && s.visibility !== "hidden" && +s.opacity > 0.05;
    }
    /* the SoloFoundry Assistant floats bottom-right on the free demos: keep the stack above its bubble,
       and step aside while its chat panel — or any menu that locks the page — is open */
    var baseLock = null;
    function scrollLocked() {
      return getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).overflow === "hidden" || getComputedStyle(document.body).overflow === "hidden";
    }
    function widgetRoot() {
      var hs = document.querySelectorAll("body > div");
      for (var i = 0; i < hs.length; i++) if (hs[i] !== host && hs[i].shadowRoot && hs[i].shadowRoot.querySelector(".bub")) return hs[i].shadowRoot;
      return null;
    }
    function place() {
      var w = widgetRoot(), b = w && w.querySelector(".bub"), pn = w && w.querySelector(".panel"), bottom = 68;
      if (b) {
        var r = b.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (r.width && r.height && r.left > window.innerWidth / 2) bottom = Math.max(bottom, Math.round(window.innerHeight - r.top + 10));
      }
      st.style.bottom = mini.style.bottom = bottom + "px";
      if (C.miniBeside) {
        var fb = document.querySelector("#__framer-badge-container a"), fr = fb && fb.getBoundingClientRect();
        if (!b && fr && fr.width && window.innerWidth >= 720 && window.innerHeight >= 480) {
          var mb = Math.max(0, Math.round(window.innerHeight - fr.bottom)), mr = Math.round(window.innerWidth - fr.left + (C.miniGap || 10)), mh = 38, mw = mini.offsetWidth || 143;
          if (C.avoid) {
            var av = document.querySelectorAll(C.avoid), ml = window.innerWidth - mr - mw;
            for (var k = 0; k < av.length; k++) {
              var ar = av[k].getBoundingClientRect(), mt = window.innerHeight - mb - mh;
              if (!ar.width || ar.right <= ml || ar.left >= ml + mw || ar.bottom <= mt || ar.top >= mt + mh) continue;
              if (window.innerHeight - ar.bottom >= mh + 7) mb = Math.min(mb, Math.round(window.innerHeight - ar.bottom - mh - 4));
              else mb = Math.round(window.innerHeight - ar.top + 8);
            }
          }
          mini.style.bottom = mb + "px"; mini.style.right = mr + "px";
        }
        else mini.style.right = "";
      }
      if (C.avoidPhone && C.avoid && window.innerWidth < 720) {
        var pv = document.querySelectorAll(C.avoid), ph = 38, pw = mini.offsetWidth || 143, pb = bottom, pl = window.innerWidth - 20 - pw;
        for (var q = 0; q < pv.length; q++) {
          var pr = pv[q].getBoundingClientRect(), pt = window.innerHeight - pb - ph;
          if (!pr.width || pr.right <= pl || pr.left >= pl + pw || pr.bottom <= pt || pr.top >= pt + ph) continue;
          pb = Math.round(window.innerHeight - pr.top + 8);
        }
        mini.style.bottom = pb + "px";
      }
      var away = !!(pn && pn.classList.contains("open") && pn.getBoundingClientRect().height > 0) || (baseLock === false && ov.hidden && scrollLocked());
      st.classList.toggle("away", away);
      mini.classList.toggle("away", away);
    }
    function showStack() { /* never "reveal": that is the card's scroll-reveal below (09-11 name-collision bug) */
      if (holding()) { setTimeout(showStack, 500); return; }
      if (ss("sfbar4-min") === "1") collapse(true);
      else if (C.startMini || window.innerHeight < 480 || window.innerWidth < 720) collapse(true, false); /* landscape phones start as the small pill */
      else st.classList.add("in");
      baseLock = scrollLocked();
      place();
      setInterval(place, 600);
      shelf();
    }
    setTimeout(showStack, reduce ? 0 : wait);
  }

  /* build on "load", or 2.5s after the DOM is ready — some demos never fire load (CAIRN /stay/the-bothy) */
  var built = false;
  function go() { if (!built) { built = true; build(); } }
  if (document.readyState === "complete") go();
  else {
    window.addEventListener("load", go);
    if (document.readyState === "interactive") setTimeout(go, 2500);
    else document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () { setTimeout(go, 2500); });
  }
})();

</script>

<!-- SnippetEnd: cE09KuezH -->

    <!-- End of bodyEnd -->
</body>
</html>
